.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:name: Update EC2
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:on:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:    push:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:        branches: ["main"] # or your chosen branch(es)
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:##"ssh -i "login-cred.pem" ubuntu@ec2-54-67-6-5.us-west-1.compute.amazonaws.com"
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:jobs:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:    deploy:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:        runs-on: ubuntu-latest
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:        steps:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:            # 2. Use SSH action to connect to EC2 and run commands
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:            - name: SSH Remote Commands
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:              uses: appleboy/ssh-action@master
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:              with:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                  host: ec2-13-52-237-125.us-west-1.compute.amazonaws.com
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                  username: ubuntu
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                  key: ${{ secrets.EC2_KEY }}
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                  script: |
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      export NVM_DIR=~/.nvm
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      source ~/.nvm/nvm.sh
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      pm2 -v
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      cd ~/CSE327-Networking
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      mkdir -p ~/.ssh
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      echo "${{ secrets.GIT_PASSWORD }}" > ~/.ssh/github_deploy_key
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      chmod 600 ~/.ssh/github_deploy_key
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      # Configure SSH to use this key for GitHub
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      cat > ~/.ssh/config << EOF
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      Host github.com
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                        IdentityFile ~/.ssh/github_deploy_key
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                        StrictHostKeyChecking no
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      EOF
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      # Make sure Git uses SSH
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      git remote set-url origin git@github.com:tortooga2/CSE327-Networking.git
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      git pull
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      npm run setup
.github/workflows/update_ec2.yaml:                      pm2 restart all

Modules/module.auth.js:        //get session token from cookie (UUID).
Modules/module.auth.js:        console.log(req.cookies);
Modules/module.auth.js:        const sessionToken = req.cookies.sessionToken;
Modules/module.auth.js:        console.log("Received session token: ", sessionToken);
Modules/module.auth.js:        if (!sessionToken) {
Modules/module.auth.js:            console.log("Session token not found");
Modules/module.auth.js:            return res
Modules/module.auth.js:                .status(404)
Modules/module.auth.js:                .json({ error: "Could not find sessionToken" }); //Redirect to login page
Modules/module.auth.js:        }
Modules/module.auth.js:
Modules/module.auth.js:        var user = null;
Modules/module.auth.js:        try {
Modules/module.auth.js:            //Get profile_id from session token (UUID).
Modules/module.auth.js:
Modules/module.auth.js:            const userString = await auth.sessionDB.get(sessionToken);
Modules/module.auth.js:            console.log("userString: ", userString);
Modules/module.auth.js:
Modules/module.auth.js:            if (!userString) {
Modules/module.auth.js:                console.log("Profile ID not found");
Modules/module.auth.js:                return res.status(404).json({
Modules/module.auth.js:                    //Redirect to login page
Modules/module.auth.js:                    message: "The server could not find profile id",
Modules/module.auth.js:            }
Modules/module.auth.js:            console.log("user: ", userString);
Modules/module.auth.js:            const userJSON = JSON.parse(userString);
Modules/module.auth.js:            user = userJSON;
Modules/module.auth.js:        } catch (error) {
Modules/module.auth.js:            debugError(error);
Modules/module.auth.js:                message: "The server encountered an unexpected condition",
Modules/module.auth.js:        }
Modules/module.auth.js:
Modules/module.auth.js:        //reset the expire time of the session token
Modules/module.auth.js:        await auth.sessionDB.expire(
Modules/module.auth.js:            sessionToken,
Modules/module.auth.js:            Number(process.env.TOKEN_EXP)
Modules/module.auth.js:        );
Modules/module.auth.js:
Modules/module.auth.js:        //Add the profile_id to the request object to be used in the route.
Modules/module.auth.js:
Modules/module.auth.js:        req.user = user;
Modules/module.auth.js:
Modules/module.auth.js:        next();
Modules/module.auth.js:    },
Modules/module.auth.js:};

Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js://Generate a random username for a user
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:const generateUsername = async (Users, desiredUsername) => {
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:    const cleanUsername = desiredUsername
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:        .toLowerCase()
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:        .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "")
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:        .trim();
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:    //Check if the username is already taken
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:    for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:        const randomSuffix = Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:            .toString()
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:            .padStart(4, "0");
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:        const existingUser = await Users.findOne({
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:            where: {
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:                username_base: cleanUsername,
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:                username_suffix: randomSuffix,
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:            },
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:        });
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:        if (!existingUser) {
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:            return {
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:                username_base: cleanUsername,
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:                username_suffix: randomSuffix,
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:            };
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:        }
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:    }
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:    return null;
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:};
Modules/module.usernameGenerator.js:
